## Best date plans

### 2026-10-17 — Open House New York: Grand Army Plaza Arch Tours + Sofreh

**Open House New York: Grand Army Plaza Arch Tours, then a meal.** Persian at Sofreh. It is a 7-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 10 am, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=Grand+Army+Plaza%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11238&destination=75+St.+Marks+Avenue%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11217&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.prospectpark.org/event/ohny-soldiers-and-sailors-memorial-arch-tours/) · [Restaurant](https://www.sofrehnyc.com/)

### 2026-10-17 — Brooklyn Navy Yard Annual Open House + Straker's

### 2026-10-12 — Radio Bakery + Margo Cilker + Lilly Hiatt

**Dinner, then Margo Cilker + Lilly Hiatt.** Bakery / cafe at Radio Bakery. It is 1.6 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. Margo Cilker + Lilly Hiatt starts at 7 pm, so book the table early.

**Dinner, then Keznamdi, GZA/Genius, The Phunky Nomads, and GZA.** Bakery / cafe at Radio Bakery. It is a 14-minute walk from the venue. Keznamdi, GZA/Genius, The Phunky Nomads, and GZA starts at 8 pm, so book the table early.

## Where to eat

Verified dining picks emphasize dated festival meals, restaurant-owned prix fixe menus and genuinely recent openings; duplicate or bundled cards were cut where they could not be made precise without changing identity.

Dining research: 2026-10-12T10:35:54+00:00

No Michelin stars were added to restaurants. Award language is limited to what checked sources stated: NYCWFF calls Nadav Greenberg a Michelin-starred talent formerly of Shmoné and Charlie Palmer Michelin-awarded; Eater notes Michelin nods for the Texas pitmasters' prior restaurants, not for Kirbee's. No independent Michelin Guide status was inferred.

### All-you-can-eat Southern country buffet opening deal

Deals & happy hours — Good Time Country Buffet

Team behind Kisa and C as in Charlie opened a retro Southern-style buffet with fried chicken, collards, mac and cheese, rib tips and rotating dishes; useful fixed-price dinner in a high-cost neighborhood.

Price: $26.99 adults; $23.99 seniors 65+; $13.99 kids ages 4-9; desserts and boozy frozen drinks extra  
Schedule: Wednesday-Sunday 5:00 PM-10:00 PM  
Conditions: Walk-ins plus limited Resy reservations; desserts and drinks cost extra; opening article states buffet format and senior/kids pricing.  
From: 2026-09-12  
Opened: 2026-09-12  
[Source](https://ny.eater.com/news/413774/good-time-country-buffet-east-village-opening-kisa)

### Texas barbecue collaboration opens in Greenpoint

Schedule: First day 11:00 AM-2:00 PM; full opening Labor Day weekend Friday-Sunday per Eater; current regular schedule not verified
